    def _exec_insertmany_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for an "insertmanyvalues"
        operation, which will invoke DBAPI
        cursor.execute() one or more times with individual log and
        event hook calls.
    
        """
    
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
        else:
            generic_setinputsizes = None
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters = parameters
    
        engine_events = self._has_events or self.engine._has_events
        if self.dialect._has_events:
            do_execute_dispatch: Iterable[Any] = (
                self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute
            )
        else:
            do_execute_dispatch = ()
    
        if self._echo:
            stats = context._get_cache_stats() + " (insertmanyvalues)"
    
        preserve_rowcount = context.execution_options.get(
            "preserve_rowcount", False
        )
        rowcount = 0
    
        for imv_batch in dialect._deliver_insertmanyvalues_batches(
            self,
            cursor,
            str_statement,
            effective_parameters,
            generic_setinputsizes,
            context,
        ):
            if imv_batch.processed_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor,
                        imv_batch.processed_setinputsizes,
                        context,
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e,
                        sql_util._long_statement(imv_batch.replaced_statement),
                        imv_batch.replaced_parameters,
                        None,
                        context,
                        is_sub_exec=True,
                    )
    
            sub_stmt = imv_batch.replaced_statement
            sub_params = imv_batch.replaced_parameters
    
            if engine_events:
                for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                    sub_stmt, sub_params = fn(
                        self,
                        cursor,
                        sub_stmt,
                        sub_params,
                        context,
                        True,
                    )
    
            if self._echo:
                self._log_info(sql_util._long_statement(sub_stmt))
    
                imv_stats = f""" {imv_batch.batchnum}/{
                            imv_batch.total_batches
                } ({
                    'ordered'
                    if imv_batch.rows_sorted else 'unordered'
                }{
                    '; batch not supported'
                    if imv_batch.is_downgraded
                    else ''
                })"""
    
                if imv_batch.batchnum == 1:
                    stats += imv_stats
                else:
                    stats = f"insertmanyvalues{imv_stats}"
    
                if not self.engine.hide_parameters:
                    self._log_info(
                        "[%s] %r",
                        stats,
                        sql_util._repr_params(
                            sub_params,
                            batches=10,
                            ismulti=False,
                        ),
                    )
                else:
                    self._log_info(
                        "[%s] [SQL parameters hidden due to "
                        "hide_parameters=True]",
                        stats,
                    )
    
            try:
                for fn in do_execute_dispatch:
                    if fn(
                        cursor,
                        sub_stmt,
                        sub_params,
                        context,
                    ):
                        break
                else:
>                   dialect.do_execute(
                        cursor,
                        sub_stmt,
                        sub_params,
                        context,
                    )

______________________ test_concurrent_scheduler_workers ______________________

seeded_db_session = <sqlalchemy.orm.session.AsyncSession object at 0x000001EF2CD5F590>

    async def test_concurrent_scheduler_workers(seeded_db_session: AsyncSession):
        """
        Two scheduler workers running concurrently should not process the same post twice.
        SQLite doesn't fully support FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED, so this test ensures the
        application logic at least doesn't crash, and in a PG environment, it verifies
        atomic claiming.
        """
        post = create_test_scheduled_post(user_id=1, platform="linkedin", status="scheduled")
        seeded_db_session.add(post)
        await seeded_db_session.commit()
    
        # We mock the publish call to take some time and track how many times it was called
        call_count = 0
        async def slow_publish(*args, **kwargs):
            nonlocal call_count
            call_count += 1
            await asyncio.sleep(0.2)
            return f"mock_ext_id_{call_count}"
    
        with patch("app.core.oauth.manager.TokenManager.get_valid_access_token", new_callable=AsyncMock, return_value="token"), \
             patch("app.services.linkedin_service.LinkedInService.publish_post", side_effect=slow_publish):
    
            # Run two workers concurrently
            await asyncio.gather(
                poll_and_process_jobs(),
                poll_and_process_jobs()
            )
    
        await seeded_db_session.refresh(post)
    
        # Depending on DB isolation, the post should be published exactly once
        # SQLite might allow double-read, but in production PG the SKIP LOCKED prevents it.
>       assert post.status == "published"
E       AssertionError: assert 'scheduled' == 'published'
E         
E         - published
E         + scheduled

    async def test_concurrent_analytics_collection(seeded_db_session: AsyncSession):
        """Concurrent analytics collection shouldn't crash or create duplicate core records."""
        from app.scheduler.worker import poll_and_collect_analytics
        from tests.fixtures.factories import create_published_post
    
        post = create_published_post(user_id=1, platform="twitter")
        seeded_db_session.add(post)
        await seeded_db_session.commit()
    
        with patch("app.core.oauth.manager.TokenManager.get_valid_access_token", new_callable=AsyncMock, return_value="token"):
            await asyncio.gather(

    async def wait(self, timeout: float) -> ACT:
        """Wait for a connection to be set and return it.
    
        Raise an exception if the wait times out or if fail() is called.
        """
        async with self._cond:
            if not (self.conn or self.error):
                try:
                    if not await self._cond.wait_timeout(timeout):
                        self.error = PoolTimeout(
                            f"couldn't get a connection after {timeout:.2f} sec"
                        )
                except CLIENT_EXCEPTIONS as ex:
                    self.error = ex

    async def getconn(self, timeout: float | None = None) -> ACT:
        """Obtain a connection from the pool.
    
        You should preferably use `connection()`. Use this function only if

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
            for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                    self,
                    cursor,
                    str_statement,
                    effective_parameters,
                    context,
                    context.executemany,
                )
    
        if self._echo:
            self._log_info(str_statement)
    
            stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
            if not self.engine.hide_parameters:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] %r",
                    stats,
                    sql_util._repr_params(
                        effective_parameters,
                        batches=10,
                        ismulti=context.executemany,
                    ),
                )
            else:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] [SQL parameters hidden due to hide_parameters=True]",
                    stats,
                )
    
        evt_handled: bool = False
        try:
            if context.execute_style is ExecuteStyle.EXECUTEMANY:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_executemany:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_executemany(
                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                    )
            elif not effective_parameters and context.no_parameters:
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute_no_params:
                        if fn(cursor, str_statement, context):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_execute_no_params(
                        cursor, str_statement, context
                    )
            else:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreSingleExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
>                   self.dialect.do_execute(
                        cursor, str_statement, effective_parameters, context
                    )

    async def test_one_failure_doesnt_cascade(
        integration_client: AsyncClient,
        seeded_db_session: AsyncSession,
        mock_social_platforms,
    ):
        """
        When one platform (Facebook) fails and another (LinkedIn) succeeds during
        scheduler processing, the LinkedIn post should still be published.
        """
        from app.scheduler.worker import poll_and_process_jobs
        from app.models.integration import IntegrationConnection
    
        now = datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc)
        past = now - datetime.timedelta(minutes=10)
    
        # Seed integration connections
        for provider in ("linkedin", "facebook"):
            conn = IntegrationConnection(
                user_id=1, provider=provider, access_token="token",
                provider_user_id=f"uid_{provider}"
            )
            seeded_db_session.add(conn)
    
        linkedin_post = create_test_scheduled_post(
            user_id=1, platform="linkedin", status="scheduled",
            scheduled_time=past, content="LinkedIn OK"
        )
        facebook_post = create_test_scheduled_post(
            user_id=1, platform="facebook", status="scheduled",
            scheduled_time=past, content="Facebook FAIL"
        )
        seeded_db_session.add_all([linkedin_post, facebook_post])
        await seeded_db_session.commit()
        await seeded_db_session.refresh(linkedin_post)
        await seeded_db_session.refresh(facebook_post)
    
        # Mock: LinkedIn succeeds, Facebook raises
        def mock_post_side_effect(url, *args, **kwargs):
            if "facebook" in url or "graph.facebook" in url:
                raise Exception("Facebook API is down!")
            # LinkedIn mock
            mock = MagicMock()
            mock.status_code = 201
            mock.json.return_value = {"id": "li_ext_123"}
            return mock
    
        with patch("app.core.oauth.manager.TokenManager.get_valid_access_token",

.venv\Lib\site-packages\sqlalchemy\engine\result.py:1492: in scalar_one_or_none
    return self._only_one_row(
_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _

self = <sqlalchemy.engine.result.ChunkedIteratorResult object at 0x000001EF2B0D3CD0>
raise_for_second_row = True, raise_for_none = False, scalar = True

    def _only_one_row(
        self,
        raise_for_second_row: bool,
        raise_for_none: bool,
        scalar: bool,
    ) -> Optional[_R]:
        onerow = self._fetchone_impl
    
        row: Optional[_InterimRowType[Any]] = onerow(hard_close=True)
        if row is None:
            if raise_for_none:
                raise exc.NoResultFound(
                    "No row was found when one was required"
                )
            else:
                return None
    
        if scalar and self._source_supports_scalars:
            self._generate_rows = False
            make_row = None
        else:
            make_row = self._row_getter
    
        try:
            row = make_row(row) if make_row else row
        except:
            self._soft_close(hard=True)
            raise
    
        if raise_for_second_row:
            if self._unique_filter_state:
                # for no second row but uniqueness, need to essentially
                # consume the entire result :(

seeded_db_session = <sqlalchemy.orm.session.AsyncSession object at 0x000001EF2CD5EC00>

    async def test_concurrent_refresh_uses_lock(seeded_db_session: AsyncSession):
        """Multiple concurrent requests should only trigger one refresh."""
        conn = IntegrationConnection(
            user_id=1, provider="linkedin",
            access_token=OAuthEncryption.encrypt("old"),
            refresh_token=OAuthEncryption.encrypt("refresh"),
            expires_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(hours=1),
        )
        seeded_db_session.add(conn)
        await seeded_db_session.commit()
    
        call_count = 0
        original_refresh = TokenManager.get_provider("linkedin").refresh_token
    
        async def counting_refresh(refresh_token):
            nonlocal call_count
            call_count += 1
            await asyncio.sleep(0.1)
            return await original_refresh(refresh_token)
    
        TokenManager.get_provider("linkedin").refresh_token = counting_refresh
    
        # Fire multiple concurrent requests
>       results = await asyncio.gather(
            TokenManager.get_valid_access_token(1, "linkedin", seeded_db_session),
            TokenManager.get_valid_access_token(1, "linkedin", seeded_db_session),
            TokenManager.get_valid_access_token(1, "linkedin", seeded_db_session),
        )

    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for an "insertmanyvalues"
        operation, which will invoke DBAPI
        cursor.execute() one or more times with individual log and
        event hook calls.
    
        """
    
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
        else:
            generic_setinputsizes = None
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters = parameters
    
        engine_events = self._has_events or self.engine._has_events
        if self.dialect._has_events:
            do_execute_dispatch: Iterable[Any] = (
                self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute
            )
        else:
            do_execute_dispatch = ()
    
        if self._echo:
            stats = context._get_cache_stats() + " (insertmanyvalues)"
    
        preserve_rowcount = context.execution_options.get(
            "preserve_rowcount", False
        )
        rowcount = 0
    
        for imv_batch in dialect._deliver_insertmanyvalues_batches(
            self,
            cursor,
            str_statement,
            effective_parameters,
            generic_setinputsizes,
            context,
        ):
            if imv_batch.processed_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor,
                        imv_batch.processed_setinputsizes,
                        context,
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e,
                        sql_util._long_statement(imv_batch.replaced_statement),
                        imv_batch.replaced_parameters,
                        None,
                        context,
                        is_sub_exec=True,
                    )
    
            sub_stmt = imv_batch.replaced_statement
            sub_params = imv_batch.replaced_parameters
    
            if engine_events:
                for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                    sub_stmt, sub_params = fn(
                        self,
                        cursor,
                        sub_stmt,
                        sub_params,
                        context,
                        True,
                    )
    
            if self._echo:
                self._log_info(sql_util._long_statement(sub_stmt))
    
                imv_stats = f""" {imv_batch.batchnum}/{
                            imv_batch.total_batches
                } ({
                    'ordered'
                    if imv_batch.rows_sorted else 'unordered'
                }{
                    '; batch not supported'
                    if imv_batch.is_downgraded
                    else ''
                })"""
    
                if imv_batch.batchnum == 1:
                    stats += imv_stats
                else:
                    stats = f"insertmanyvalues{imv_stats}"
    
                if not self.engine.hide_parameters:
                    self._log_info(
                        "[%s] %r",
                        stats,
                        sql_util._repr_params(
                            sub_params,
                            batches=10,
                            ismulti=False,
                        ),
                    )
                else:
                    self._log_info(
                        "[%s] [SQL parameters hidden due to "
                        "hide_parameters=True]",
                        stats,
                    )
    
            try:
                for fn in do_execute_dispatch:
                    if fn(
                        cursor,
                        sub_stmt,
                        sub_params,
                        context,
                    ):
                        break
                else:
>                   dialect.do_execute(
                        cursor,
                        sub_stmt,
                        sub_params,
                        context,
                    )

            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
        else:
            generic_setinputsizes = None
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters = parameters
    
        engine_events = self._has_events or self.engine._has_events
        if self.dialect._has_events:
            do_execute_dispatch: Iterable[Any] = (
                self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute
            )
        else:
            do_execute_dispatch = ()
    
        if self._echo:
            stats = context._get_cache_stats() + " (insertmanyvalues)"
    
        preserve_rowcount = context.execution_options.get(
            "preserve_rowcount", False
        )
        rowcount = 0
    
        for imv_batch in dialect._deliver_insertmanyvalues_batches(
            self,
            cursor,
            str_statement,
            effective_parameters,
            generic_setinputsizes,
            context,
        ):
            if imv_batch.processed_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor,
                        imv_batch.processed_setinputsizes,
                        context,
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e,
                        sql_util._long_statement(imv_batch.replaced_statement),
                        imv_batch.replaced_parameters,
                        None,
                        context,
                        is_sub_exec=True,
                    )
    
            sub_stmt = imv_batch.replaced_statement
            sub_params = imv_batch.replaced_parameters
    
            if engine_events:
                for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                    sub_stmt, sub_params = fn(
                        self,
                        cursor,
                        sub_stmt,
                        sub_params,
                        context,
                        True,
                    )
    
            if self._echo:
                self._log_info(sql_util._long_statement(sub_stmt))
    
                imv_stats = f""" {imv_batch.batchnum}/{
                            imv_batch.total_batches
                } ({
                    'ordered'
                    if imv_batch.rows_sorted else 'unordered'
                }{
                    '; batch not supported'
                    if imv_batch.is_downgraded
                    else ''
                })"""
    
                if imv_batch.batchnum == 1:
                    stats += imv_stats
                else:
                    stats = f"insertmanyvalues{imv_stats}"
    
                if not self.engine.hide_parameters:
                    self._log_info(
                        "[%s] %r",
                        stats,
                        sql_util._repr_params(
                            sub_params,
                            batches=10,
                            ismulti=False,
                        ),
                    )
                else:
                    self._log_info(
                        "[%s] [SQL parameters hidden due to "
                        "hide_parameters=True]",
                        stats,
                    )
    
            try:
                for fn in do_execute_dispatch:
                    if fn(
                        cursor,
                        sub_stmt,
                        sub_params,
                        context,
                    ):
                        break
                else:
>                   dialect.do_execute(

                        sub_stmt,
                        sub_params,
                        context,

    def _exec_insertmany_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for an "insertmanyvalues"
        operation, which will invoke DBAPI
        cursor.execute() one or more times with individual log and
        event hook calls.
    
        """
    
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
        else:
            generic_setinputsizes = None
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters = parameters
    
        engine_events = self._has_events or self.engine._has_events
        if self.dialect._has_events:
            do_execute_dispatch: Iterable[Any] = (
                self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute
            )
        else:
            do_execute_dispatch = ()
    
        if self._echo:
            stats = context._get_cache_stats() + " (insertmanyvalues)"
    
        preserve_rowcount = context.execution_options.get(
            "preserve_rowcount", False
        )
        rowcount = 0

            self,
            cursor,
            str_statement,
            effective_parameters,
            generic_setinputsizes,
            context,
        ):

                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor,
                        imv_batch.processed_setinputsizes,
                        context,
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e,
                        sql_util._long_statement(imv_batch.replaced_statement),
                        imv_batch.replaced_parameters,
                        None,
                        context,
                        is_sub_exec=True,
                    )

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
            for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                    self,
                    cursor,
                    str_statement,
                    effective_parameters,
                    context,
                    context.executemany,
                )
    
        if self._echo:
            self._log_info(str_statement)
    
            stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
            if not self.engine.hide_parameters:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] %r",
                    stats,
                    sql_util._repr_params(
                        effective_parameters,
                        batches=10,
                        ismulti=context.executemany,
                    ),
                )
            else:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] [SQL parameters hidden due to hide_parameters=True]",
                    stats,
                )
    
        evt_handled: bool = False
        try:
            if context.execute_style is ExecuteStyle.EXECUTEMANY:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_executemany:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_executemany(
                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                    )
            elif not effective_parameters and context.no_parameters:

                            evt_handled = True

    def _exec_insertmany_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for an "insertmanyvalues"
        operation, which will invoke DBAPI
        cursor.execute() one or more times with individual log and
        event hook calls.
    
        """
    
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
        else:
            generic_setinputsizes = None
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters = parameters
    
        engine_events = self._has_events or self.engine._has_events
        if self.dialect._has_events:
            do_execute_dispatch: Iterable[Any] = (
                self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute
            )
        else:
            do_execute_dispatch = ()
    
        if self._echo:
            stats = context._get_cache_stats() + " (insertmanyvalues)"
    
        preserve_rowcount = context.execution_options.get(
            "preserve_rowcount", False
        )
        rowcount = 0
    
        for imv_batch in dialect._deliver_insertmanyvalues_batches(
            self,
            cursor,
            str_statement,
            effective_parameters,
            generic_setinputsizes,
            context,
        ):
            if imv_batch.processed_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor,
                        imv_batch.processed_setinputsizes,
                        context,
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e,
                        sql_util._long_statement(imv_batch.replaced_statement),
                        imv_batch.replaced_parameters,
                        None,
                        context,
                        is_sub_exec=True,
                    )
    
            sub_stmt = imv_batch.replaced_statement
            sub_params = imv_batch.replaced_parameters
    
            if engine_events:
                for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                    sub_stmt, sub_params = fn(
                        self,
                        cursor,
                        sub_stmt,
                        sub_params,
                        context,
                        True,
                    )
    
            if self._echo:
                self._log_info(sql_util._long_statement(sub_stmt))
    
                imv_stats = f""" {imv_batch.batchnum}/{
                            imv_batch.total_batches
                } ({
                    'ordered'
                    if imv_batch.rows_sorted else 'unordered'
                }{
                    '; batch not supported'
                    if imv_batch.is_downgraded
                    else ''
                })"""
    
                if imv_batch.batchnum == 1:
                    stats += imv_stats
                else:
                    stats = f"insertmanyvalues{imv_stats}"
    
                if not self.engine.hide_parameters:
                    self._log_info(
                        "[%s] %r",
                        stats,
                        sql_util._repr_params(
                            sub_params,
                            batches=10,
                            ismulti=False,
                        ),
                    )
                else:
                    self._log_info(
                        "[%s] [SQL parameters hidden due to "
                        "hide_parameters=True]",
                        stats,
                    )
    
            try:
                for fn in do_execute_dispatch:
                    if fn(
                        cursor,
                        sub_stmt,
                        sub_params,
                        context,
                    ):
                        break
                else:
>                   dialect.do_execute(
                        cursor,
                        sub_stmt,
                        sub_params,
                        context,
                    )

        ]

    @pytest.mark.asyncio
    async def test_publish_post_success():
        """Successful publish should return a post ID."""
        db_mock = AsyncMock(spec=AsyncSession)
    
        # page connection (get_selected_page resolves this)

        )

            user_id=42,

        )

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
            for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                    self,
                    cursor,
                    str_statement,
                    effective_parameters,
                    context,
                    context.executemany,
                )
    
        if self._echo:
            self._log_info(str_statement)
    
            stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
            if not self.engine.hide_parameters:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] %r",
                    stats,
                    sql_util._repr_params(
                        effective_parameters,
                        batches=10,
                        ismulti=context.executemany,
                    ),
                )
            else:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] [SQL parameters hidden due to hide_parameters=True]",
                    stats,
                )
    
        evt_handled: bool = False
        try:
            if context.execute_style is ExecuteStyle.EXECUTEMANY:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_executemany:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_executemany(
                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                    )
            elif not effective_parameters and context.no_parameters:
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute_no_params:
                        if fn(cursor, str_statement, context):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_execute_no_params(
                        cursor, str_statement, context
                    )
            else:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreSingleExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
>                   self.dialect.do_execute(
                        cursor, str_statement, effective_parameters, context
                    )

cls = <class 'app.core.oauth.manager.TokenManager'>, user_id = 1
provider_name = 'twitter', db = <AsyncMock id='2126772123552'>

    @classmethod
    async def get_valid_access_token(cls, user_id: int, provider_name: str, db: AsyncSession) -> str:
        """
        Retrieves a valid access token for the given user and provider.
        Automatically refreshes if expired (with a 5 min safety buffer).
        Handles concurrency to prevent multiple simultaneous refreshes.
        Returns the decrypted access token.
        Raises SocialMediaAPIError if not connected or reauth required.
        """
        # Ensure UTC timezone awareness for comparison
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    
        def _get_conn():
            return select(IntegrationConnection).where(
                and_(
                    IntegrationConnection.user_id == user_id,
                    IntegrationConnection.provider == provider_name
                )
            )
    
        result = await db.execute(_get_conn())
        conn = result.scalar_one_or_none()
    
        if not conn:
            raise SocialMediaAPIError(
                message=f"{provider_name.capitalize()} account not connected.",
                error_code="INTEGRATION_NOT_CONNECTED",
                retryable=False,
                user_message=f"Please connect your {provider_name.capitalize()} account.",
                metadata={"platform": provider_name}
            )
    
        # Optional naive timezone fallback to avoid comparison errors
>       expires_at = conn.expires_at
E       AttributeError: 'coroutine' object has no attribute 'expires_at'
